नियम 2 (IV ) के अनुसरण में भारतीय कृषि अनुसन्धान परिषद् 
की शामी निका के निम्नलिखित सदस्यों को 4 अगस्त , 1972 
तक की अवधि 27 यदि वे 4 अगस्त , 1972 के बाद भारतीय 
कृषि अनुसन्धान परिषद की शासी निकाय के सदस्य पुनःनियुक्त 
हो जाते हैं तो दिनांक 28 मार्च, 1972 से एक वर्ष की अवधि

चुने जाने तक, जो भी बाद में हो, शासी निकाय द्वारा स्थायी विन 
मिति के सदस्य चुन लिया गया है : - -

नई दिल्ली, 27 अप्रैल , 1972 
सा० प्रा० 1914.- भारतीय व्यापार पोत ( नाविक रोजगार 
कार्यालय, कलकत्ता ) नियम , 195 4 के नियम 5 के अनुसरण में 
केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्वारा श्री टी० बागवी को श्री पार०एन० . 
घोसाल , जिन्होंने त्याग पत्र दे दिया है, के शेष कार्य काल के लिए , 
पोतमालिकों का प्रतिनिधित्व करने हेतु कलकत्ता पत्तन पर नाविक 
रोजगार बोर्ड (विदेशगामी ) का सदस्य नियुक्त करती है और 
नौवहन और परिवहन मन्त्रालय (परिवहन पक्ष ) में भारत सरकार 
की अधिसूचना सा०या०सं० 2027 दिनांक 28 - 5 - 70 में 
निम्नलिखित संशोधन करती है, अर्थात् : 

उक्त अधिसूचना में क्रम सं० 9 में प्रविष्टि " श्री पार०एन० 

घोसाल " के स्थान पर , प्रविष्टि " श्री टी० बागची " 
प्रतिस्थापित की जायेगी ।

amount for which a bond has to be given by the master 
of a ship under the said sub -section ) snall be exercis 
ahle also by the Director General of Shinping.

केन्द्रीय प्रत्यक्ष कर बोर्ड 

( मायकर ) 
नई दिल्ली , 25 मार्च, 1972 
एस० प्रो० 1919. - - अधिसूचना सं० 353, तारीख 
17--12 - 71 द्वारा यथा पुनरीक्षित अधिसूचना सं० 
258, तारीख 1 - 9 - 71 को भागतः उपान्तरित 
करते हुए और प्रायकर अधिनियम , 1961 ( 1961 का 43 ) 
की धारा 121 की उप -धारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग 
करते हुए, केन्द्रीय प्रत्यक्ष कर बोर्ड एतद्वारा निदेश देता है कि 
अधिसूचना सं० 87 [ फा० सं० 187/ 13/ 70 - आई० टी० ( ए-I )] 
तारीख 29 - 5 - 70 के उपाबन्ध ख में यथा उपणित , ( केन्द्रीय ) 
कलकत्ता चार्ज की बाबत , अपर प्रायकर आयुक्त ( वसूली ) के 
कुत्य , जो अब तक अपर प्रायकर आयुक्त वसूली - 2, कलकत्ता 
द्वारा किए जाते थे, 17-12- 71 से आयकर आयुक्त ( केन्द्रीय ) 
कलकत्ता द्वारा किए जाएंगे । 
[ स० 64/ फा० स० 187/ 8/ 71--प्राईटी० ( ए ०-I ) 

बी० माधवन, अवर सचिव । 


वसूली से सम्बन्धित , कार्य जैसा कि आयकर अधिनियम , 1961 
के अध्याय XVII में विस्तृत रूप से दिया गया है तथा निम्नलिखित

जाने पर भी , अपनी इस असफलता के लिए कोई कारण अथवा 
स्पष्टीकरण नही दिया है, और निर्वाचन आयोग का यह भी समा 
धान हो गया है कि उसके पास इस असफलता के लिए कोई पर्याप्त 
कारण या न्यायौचित्य नहीं है , 

अत : अब , उक्त अधिनियम को धारा 10-2 के अनुसरण 
में निर्वाचन आयोग एतद्वारा उक्त श्री एम० रमैय्या को संसद 
के किसी भी सदन के या किसी राज्य की विधान सभा अथवा विधान 
परिषद् के सदस्य चुने जाने और होने के लिए, इस आदेश की ता . 
से तीन वर्ष की कालावधि के लिए निरहित घोषित करता है । 

[ सं० केरल-वि० स०/ 79/ 70 ]

तधीन बनाए गए नियमों द्वारा यथा अपेक्षित अपने निर्वाचन 
ध्ययों का कोई भी लेख दाखिल करने में असफल रहे हैं ; 


और, यतः उक्त उम्मीदवार ने, उमे सभ्यक मुचना दिये जाने 
पर भी अपनी इम असफलता के लिए कोई कारण अथवा स्पाटी 
करण नहीं दिया है, और निर्वाचन आयोग का यह भी समाधान 
हो गया है कि उसके पास इस अमफलता के लिए कोई पर्याप्त कारण 
या न्यायोचित नहीं है , 

अतः, अब , उक्त अधिनियम को धारा 10- क के अनुमरण में 
निर्वाचन आयोग एतद्द्वारा उक्त श्री मून्दरम मुरुगाडे को संसद 
के किसी भी सदन के या किसी राज्य की विधान राभा अथवा विधान 
परिषद् के सदस्य चुने जाने और होने के लिए, इस आदेश की तारीख 
से तीन वर्ष की कालावधि के लिए निरहित घोषित करता है ।

दाखिल करने में असफज रहें हैं ;

S . O . 1954 , — Whereas it appears to the Central Gov 
ernment that the employer and the majority of the 
eniployees in relation to the establishment known as 
Messrs Associated Commerce and Industries ( India ) 
Pilvate Limited , 12 , Netaji Subhas Road , Calcutta - 1

का० प्रा० 2030.- कर्मचारी राज्य बीमा अधिनियम, 1948 
( 1948 का 34 ) की धारा 73--च द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग 
करते हुए और भारत सरकार के श्रम , रोजगार और पुनर्वास 
मंत्रालय ( श्रम और रोजगार विभाग ) की अधिसूचना सं० का० 


का० प्रा०2031. - यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार का यह समाधान हो 
गया था कि मैसूर सीमेंटस लिमिटेड, आमासंद्रा क्षेत्र में स्थित था 
जो मैसूर राज्य के तुमकुर जिले में बिखरी हुई आबादी का क्षेत्र 
( अर्थात् ऐमा क्षेत्र जिसकी बीमा योग्य प्राबादी 500 से कम थी ) 
था । 

और , यत : उसकी बिखरी हुई आबादी के क्षेत्र में अवस्थिति 
के आधार पर केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उपर्युक्त कारखाने को , भारत 
सरकार के भूतपूर्व सामाजिक सुरक्षा विभाग की अधिसूचना सं० का० 
प्रा० 946 नारीख 19 मार्च, 1965, द्वारा कर्मचारी राज्य बीमा 
अधिनियम , 1948 ( 1948 का 34) की धारा 73च के अधीन 
नियोजक के विशेष अभिदाय के संदाय मे तब तक के लिए छूट दे दी 
थी जब तक कि उस अधिनियम के अध्याय 5 के उपबंध उस क्षेत्र में 
प्रवर्तित नहीं हो जाते । 

और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार का यह समाधान हो गया है कि 
मैसूर राज्य के तुमकुर जिले में प्रामासंद्रा क्षेत्र की बीमा योग्य 
आबादी अब 500 से बढ़ गई है, और वह अब बिखरी हुई प्राबादी 
का क्षेत्र नहीं है । 


सीवेज प्योरिफिकेशन प्लान्ट , कोलाबा, मुम्बई की ऐसी क्षेत्र 
में , जिसमें उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 4 और 5 के उपबंध 
प्रवृत्त है, अवस्थिति को ध्यान में रखते हुए उक्त संयन्त्र को उक्त 
अधिनियम के अध्याय 5 - क के प्रधधोन उद्ग्रहणीय नियोजक 
के विशेष अभिदाय के संदाय से उक्त अधिसूचना में विनिर्दिष्ट 
अवधि की समाप्ति की तारीख से 2 अप्रैल , 1973 तक जिस में यह 
दिन भी सम्मिलित है , एक और वर्ष की कालावधि के 
लिए एतद्वारा छूट देती है ।

का० प्रा० 2033 ---कर्मचारी राज्य बीमा अधिनियम , 1948 ( 1948 का 34 ) की धारा 73 - च द्वारा प्रदन शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते 
हुए और भारत सरकार के श्रम रोजगार और पूनममंत्रालय ( श्रम और रोजगार विभाग ) की अधिसूचना संख्या का० प्रा० 2970 तारीख 17 
जुलाई , 1971 के क्रम में केन्द्रीय मरकार इमसे उपाबद्ध अनुसूची के स्तम्भ ( 4 ) में विनिर्दिष्ट कारखानों की उक्न अनुसूची के स्तम्भ ( 3 ) 
में विनिर्दिष्ट उत्तर प्रदेश राज्य के ऐसे क्षेत्रों में , जिसमें उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 4 और 5 के उपबन्ध प्रवृत्त नहीं है, अवस्थिति को ध्यान में 
रखते हुए उक्त कारखानों को उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 5 - क के अधीन उद्ग्रहणीय नियोजक के विशेष अभिदाय के सन्दाय मे , उक्त अधिसूचना 
में विनिर्दिष्ट अवधि की समाप्ति की तारीख मे एक वर्ष की अवधि के लिए या तब तक के लिए जब तक कि उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 5 
के उपबन्ध उन क्षेत्रों में प्रकृत नहीं हो जाते , जो भी पहले हो , उसके लिए , एतद्वारा छूट देती है ।

का० प्रा० 2034.- कर्मचारी राज्य बीमा अधिनियम , 1948 ( 1948 का 34 ) की धारा 73 - च द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते 
हए और भारत सरकार के श्रम और पुनर्वास मंत्रालय ( श्रम और रोजगार विभाग ) की अधिसूचना संख्या का० प्रा० 5 309 तारीख 4 नवम्बर , 
1971 के क्रम में केन्द्रीय सरकार इससे उपाबद्ध अनुसूची के स्तम्भ ( 4 ) में विनिर्दिष्ट कारखाने की , उक्त अनुसूची के स्तम्भ ( 3 ) में विनिर्दिष्ट 
पंजाब राज्य के ऐसे क्षेत्रों में , जिसमें उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 4 और 5 के उपबन्ध प्रवृत्त नहीं है , अवस्थति को ध्यान में रखते हुए , उक्त कारखाने 
को , उक्त प्रधिनयिम के अध्याय 5- क के अधीन उग्रहणीय नियोजकों के विशेष अभिदाय के सन्दाय से उक्त अधिसूचना में विनिर्दिष्ट कालावधि 
के अवसान की तारीख से 4 जूलाई 1972 तक , जिसमें यह दिन भी सम्मिलित है, एक वर्ष की अवधि के लिए या तब तक के लिए जब तक उक्त 
अधिनियम के मध्याय 5- के उपबन्ध उन क्षेत्रों में प्रवृत्त नहीं हो जाते, जो भी पहले हो, एतद्द्वारा छूट देती है ।

का०मा० 2035. -- कर्मचारी राज्य बीमा अधिनियम , 1948 ( 1948 का 34 ) को धारा 73- च द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग 
करते हुए और भारत सरकार के श्रम , रोजगार और पुनर्वास मंत्रालय ( श्रम और रोजगार विभाग ) की अधिसूचना संख्या का० प्रा० 2971 तारीख 
17 जुलाई, 1971 के क्रम में केन्द्रीय सरकार इससे उपाबद्ध अनुसूची के स्तम्भ ( 4 ) में विनिर्दिष्ट कारखानों की उक्त अनुसूची के स्तम्भ 
( 3 ) में विनिर्दिष्ट उत्तर प्रदेश राज्य के ऐसे क्षेत्रों में , जिसमें उक्त अभिनियम के अध्याय 4 और 5 के उपबन्ध प्रवृत्त नहीं है, अवस्थिति को 
ध्यान में रखते हए उक्त कारखानों को उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 5 - क के अधीन उद्ग्रहणीय नियोजक के विशिष अभिदाय के संदाय से, उक्त 
अधिसूचना में विनिर्दिष्ट अवधि की समाप्ति की तारीख से एक वर्ष की अवधि के लिए या तब तक के लिए जब तक कि उक्न अधिनियम के 
अध्याय 5 के उपबन्ध उन क्षेत्रों में प्रवृत्त नहीं हो जाते , जो भी तहले हो उसके लिए, एसद्वारा छूट देती है ।

का० प्रा० 2036.- कर्मचारी राज्य बीमा अधिनियम, 1948 ( 1948 का 34) की धारा 73 च द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग 
करते हुए और भारत सरकार के श्रम और पुनर्वास मंत्रालय ( श्रम और रोजगार विभाग ) की अधिसूचना सं० का० प्रा० 2972 तारीख 
17 जुलाई , 1971 के क्रम में केन्द्रीय सरकार इससे उपाबद्ध अनुसूची के स्तम्भ ( 4 ) में विनिर्दिष्ट कारखानों कॉ उक्त अनुसूची के स्तम्भ ( 3 ) 
में विनिर्दिष्ट उत्तर प्रदेश राज्य के ऐसे क्षेत्रों में , जिसमें में उक्त अधिनियम के ,अध्याय 4 और 5 उपाबद्ध प्रवत्त नहीं हैं , अवस्थिति को ध्यान में 
रखते हुए उक्त कारखानों को उम्पत अधिनियम के अध्याय 5 -क के उदग्रीय नियोजक के विशेष अभिदाय के संदाय से , उक्त अधिसूचना में 
विनिर्दिष्ट अविध समाप्ती की तारीख से एक वर्ष की अवधि की लिए या तब तक के लिए जब तक कि उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 5 के उपाबन्ध उन 
क्षेत्रों में प्रवृत्त नहीं हो जाते , जो भी पहले हो उसके लिए, एतदद्वारा छूट देती है ।

का० प्रा० 2037.-- - कर्मचारी राज्य बीमा अधिनियम , 1948 ( 1948 का 34 ) की धारा 73 च प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते 
हुए और भारत सरकार के श्रम, रोजगार और पुनर्वास मंत्रालय ( श्रम और रोजगार विभाग ) की अधिसूचना संख्या का० प्रा० 1142 तारीख 
22 फरवरी, 1971 के क्रम में केन्द्रीय सरकार इममे उपाबद्ध अनुसूची के स्तम्भ 4 में विदिष्ट कारखानों की उक्त अनुसूची के स्तम्भ 3 में विनिर्दिष्ट 
राजस्थान राज्य के ऐसे क्षेत्रों में , जिसमें उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 4 और 5 के उपबन्ध प्रथवृत्त नहीं हैं , अवस्थिति को ध्यान में रखते हुए ; 
उक्त कारखानों को उक्त अधिनियम अध्याय 5 क के अधीन उद्ग्रीणीय नियोजक के विशेष अभिदाय के संदाय से, राजपत्र में उक्त अधिसूचना 
में विनिर्दिष्ट प्रविधि की समाम्ति की तारीख से एक वर्ष को और अवधि के लिए या तब तक के लिए जब तक कि उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 
8 के उपमन्ध उन क्षेत्रों में प्रवृत्त नहीं हो जाते, इनमें से जो भी पहले हो , उसके लिए , एतदद्वारा छूट वेटती है ।

का० प्रा० 2033. --- कर्मचारी राज्य बीमा अधिनियम , 1948 ( 1948 का 34 ) की धारा 73 च द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते 
हए और भारत सरकार के श्रम और पुनर्वास मंत्रालय ( श्रम औररोजगार विभाग ) की अधिसूचना से० का० प्रा० 2782 तारीख 5 जुलाई , 
के क्रम में केन्द्रीय सरकार इमसे उपाबन्ध अनुसूची के स्तम्भ ( 4 ) में विनिर्दिष्ट कारखानों की , उक्त अनुसूची के स्तम्भ ( 3 ) में विनिर्दिष्ट बिहार 
बिहार के राज्य के ऐसे क्षेत्रों में जिनमें अधिनियम के अध्याय 4 और 5 के उपाबन्ध प्रवृत्त नहीं है, अवस्थिति को ध्यान में रखते हुए उक्त कारखानों 
को उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 5 के अधीन उदग्रीहणीय नियोजक के विशेष अभिदाय के दाय से, उक्त अधिसूचना में विनिर्दिष्ट अवधि की समाप्ति 
समाप्ति की तारीख से एक और वर्ष की अवधि के लिए या तब तक के लिए जब तक कि उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 5 के उपनन्ध उन क्षेत्रों में 
प्रवृत्त नहीं हो जाते इनमें से जो भी पहले हो उसके लिए , एतदद्वारा छूट देती है ।

हए उक्त कारखानो को उमस अधिनियम के प्रमाय 5 - 4 के अधीन उपाहणीय मियाज के विशेष अभिदाय के संबाय से , उक्त 
अधिसूचना में विनिर्दिष्ट प्रवधि की समाप्ति की तारीख से एक वर्ष की अवधि के लिए या तब तक के लिए जब तक कि उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय

का० मा० 2040. - -कर्मचारी राज्य बीमा अधिनियम , 1948 ( 1948. का 34 ) को धारा 73वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग 
करते उप और भारत सरकार के श्रम और रोजगार औरपुनबास मन्त्रालय ( भमा भीर रोजगारविभाग) की अधिसूचना मख्या का मा० . 1143 
सारीख 22 फरवरी, 1971 के क्रम में कन्द्रीय सरकारइससे उपाबद्ध मनु सूची के स्तम्भ 4 मे विनिर्दिष्ट कारखानों की उक्त अन सूची के सम्म 3 में 
विनिदिष्ट उत्तर प्रदेश राज्य के ऐसे क्षेत्रों में , जिसमें उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 4 भौंर 5 के उपबन्ध प्रवृत्त नहीं है , अवस्थिति को ध्यान 
में रखते हए उक्त कारखानो को उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 5 क के अधीन उदग्रोहणीय नियोजक के विशेष प्राभदाय के संदाय से उक्त प्रधि 
मचना मे विनिर्दिष्ट अवधि की समाप्ती की तारीख से एक बर्ष का और अवधि के लिए या तब तक के लिए जब तक कि उक्त अधिनियम 

अध्याय 5 के उपबन्ध उन क्षेत्रों में प्रवृत्त नही हो जाते , जो भी पहले हो . एतवारा छूट देती है ।

DALJIT SINGH, Under Secy . 
का० प्रा० 2041 - कर्मचारी राज्य बीमा अधिनियम , 1948 ( 1948 का 34) की धारा 73 च द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते 
हए और भारत सरकार के श्रम , और पुनर्वास मंत्रालय ( श्रम और रोजगार विभाग ) की अधिसूचना सं० का० प्रा० 3052 तारीख 
31 जलाई, 1971 के क्रम में केन्द्रीय सरकार इससे पाबसा अनुसूची के स्तम्भ 4 में विनिर्दिष्ट कारखानों में की उक्त अनुसूची के स्तम्भ 3 में 
विनिदिष्ट बिहार राज्य के ऐसे क्षेत्रों में जिसमें उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 4 और 5 के उपबन्ध प्रवृत्त नहीं हैं , अवस्थिति को ध्यान में रखते हए , 
उसम कारखानों को उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 5 क के अधीन उद्ग्रीहणीय नियोजक के विशेष अभिदाय के संदाय से 31 मई, 1972 से 30 माई : 
19737 . जिसमें वह दिन भी सम्मिलिति है , एक वष की और अवधि के लिए या तब तक के लिए जब तक कि उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 

के उपबन्ध उन क्षेत्रों में प्रवृत्त नहीं हो जाते , इनमें से जो भी पहले हो उसके लिए, एतदद्वारा छूट देती है ।

S . O . 2043. - In exercise of the powers conferred by 
sub - sections ( 1 ) and ( 2 ) of section 8 of the Dock 
Workers ( Regulation of Employment ) Act, 1948 ( 9 of 
1948 ) , the Central Government hereby makes the fol 
lowing rules further to amend the Dock Workers 
( Regulation of Employment ) Rules , 1962 , namely : 

1 . This Rule may be called the Dock Workers 
( Regulation of Employment ) Amendment Rules. 1972, 

2 . In the Dock Workers ( Regulation of Employment) 
Rules, 1962. to sub -rule ( 3 ) , of rule 7 the following 
proviso shall be added , namely : 
" Provided that the decision of the Board on any 

question may also be considered if the Chair 
man so directs , by circulation of the necessary 
papers to every member of the Board and if 
there is no unanimity of decision on any ques 
tion in circulation , the said question shall be 
placed before the Board for its decision , " 

[ No. F . S- 65012 / 2 / 71 - P & D. ] 


और ( 2) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार 
एसद्वारा डाक कर्मकार ( नियोजन का विनियमन ) नियम , 1962 
में और प्रागे संशोधन करने के लिए निम्नलिखित नियम बनाती 
है, अर्थात् : -- 
1 ( 1 ) ये नियम डाक कर्मकार ( नियोजन का विनियमन ) 

संशोधन नियम, 1972 कहे जा सकेंगे । 
( 2) डाक कर्मकार ( नियोजन का विनियमन ) नियम, 

1962 के नियम 7 मे, उपनियम 3 के साथ निम्न 
लिखित परन्तुक जोड़ा आएगा, अर्थात् : -- . 
" परन्तु यदि अध्यक्ष ऐसा निदेश दे तो किसी भी प्रश्न 

पर बोर्ड के विनिश्चय पर बोर्ड के प्रत्येक सदस्य को 
आवयश्क कागजता के परिचालन द्वारा विचार किया 
जा सकता है और यदि परिचालन में के किसी प्रश्न

Opinion that the minimum rates of wages should be

in respect of employment in Graphite Mines covered 
under the Mines Act , 1952 ( 35 of 1952 ) .

notice of its intention to add the said employment in 
Part I of the Schedule to the said Act.

Explanation 

1 . (a) Unskilled work is one which involves simple operations requiring little or no skill or experience on the job . 

( b ) Semi- stulled work is one which involves some degree of skill or competence required through experience on the job and which 
is capable of being performed under the supervision or guidance of skilled employce, and includes unskilled supervisory work . 

(c ) Skilled work is one which involves skill of competence acquired through experience on the job or through training as an appren 
tice or in a technical or vocational institute and the performance of which calls for initiative and judgement. 

2. The minimum rates of wages are all inclusive rates and include also the wages for weekly day of rest. 

3. Where essential commodities at concessional rates are supplied to employees , the exmployers may , if they so desire , approac 
the Central Government to get the cash value of the concessions duly computed by the competent authority .

का० प्रा० 2054. - यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि इस 
के उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारेमें भारतीय सेन्ट्रल 
बैंक से सम्बद नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक 
विवार विद्यमान है । 


पतः पय पौधोगिक विवाय पधिनियम , 1987 ( 1987 
का 14 ) की धारा 7 - क और धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 2 ) वारा 
प्रवत शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्धारा 
एक प्रौद्योगिक प्रधिकरण गठित करती है, जिस, पीठासीन अधिकारी 
श्री थिरू के सोथाराम राव होंगे जिनका मुख्यालय मद्रास में होगा 
भौर उक्त विवाद को उक्त प्रौद्योगिक प्रधिकरण को न्यायनिर्णयन 
के लिए निर्देशित करती है ।

विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1949 का 14 ) की धारा 
7 -क और धारा 33- ख को उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों

नारायण शर्मा से उक्त विवाद से सम्बद्ध कार्यवाहियों को वापस

कोलियरी, जिला शहडोल ( मध्य प्रदेश ) के प्रबन्ध से सम्बद्ध 
नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद 
विद्यमान है ;

का०मा० 2077. - यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार की गय है कि इससे 
उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में नैशनल कोल 
डेवेलपमैंट कार्पोरेशन लिमिटेड की स्वांग कोलियरी, डाकघर स्वांग , 
जिला हजारी बाग के प्रबन्ध से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उनके 
कर्मकारों के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है ; 

और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के 
लिए निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है ; 

अतः, अब , औद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 
का 14 ) की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( घ ) द्वारा 
प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए , केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्द्वारा 
उक्त विवाद को उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 7 - क के अधीन गठित 
केन्द्रीय सरकार औद्योगिक अधिकरण, ( सं० 1 ) , धनबाद को 
न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए निर्देशित करती है । 

अनुसूची 
" क्या कोलियरी मजदूर संघ की यह मांग कि मैसर्स नेशनल 

कोल डेवेलपमेंट कार्पोरेशन लिमिटेड की स्वांग 
कोलियरी , डाकघर स्वांग, जिला हजारीबाग के श्री 
मोहम्मद नजीर हुसैन , प्रोवर मैन की ज्ये - ठ 
प्रोवरमैन के रूप में भूतलक्षी प्रभाव से प्रोन्नति 
की जानी चाहिए, न्यायोचित है ? यदि हां , 


का० प्रा० 2078. - - यत :मैसर्स राष्ट्रीय खदान मजदूर सहकारी 
समिति , ठेकेदार, कोकान खान , डाकघर डाल्ली राजहारा , दुर्ग 
( मध्य प्रदेश ) के प्रबन्ध से सम्बन्ध नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों , 
जिनका प्रतिनिधित्व संयुक्त खदान मजदूर संघ , डाकघर डाल्ली 

राजहारा, दुर्ग ( मध्य प्रदेश ) करता है , ने प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधि 
नियम , 1947 ( 1947 का 14 ) की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 2 ) 
के अधीन संयुक्त रुप से केन्द्रीय सरकार को आवेदन किया है कि वह 
उनके बीच विद्यमान प्रौद्योगिक विवाद को उक्त भावेदन में उप 
वर्णित और इस से उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में उद्धृत विषय के बारे में 
किसी प्रौद्योगिक अधिकरन को निर्देशित करे । 

और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार का समाधान हो गया है कि आवेदन 
करने वाले व्यक्ति प्रत्येक पक्ष के बहुमत का प्रतिनिधित्व करते हैं । 

अतः, अब , औद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 
का 14 ) की धारा 10 की उमवारा ( 2 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों 
का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्वारा उक्त विवाद को 
अधिनियम की धारा 7 - क के अधीन गठित केन्द्रीय सरकार औयो 
गिक अधिकरण, जबलपुर को न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए निर्देशित 
करती है ।

S . O . 2079 . - In pursuance of section 17 of the Indus 
trial Disputes Act , 1947 ( 14 of 1947 ) , the Central 
Government hereby publishes the following award of 
the Central Government Industrial Tribunal ( No . 2 ) , 
Bombay , in the industrial dispute between the em 
ployers in relation to the management of Messi s 
Chowgulę and Company Private Limited , Mormugao 
Harbour (Goa ) and their workmen , wliich was re 
ceived by the Central Government on the 18th July , 
1972 . 


undated resignation alleged to have been re 
ceived on 3rd June, 1971 was justified ? If 

not , to what relief is the workınan entitled ? " 
2 . After the receipt of this reference notices were 
issued to the parties . In pursuance of this notice M / s . 
Chowgule & Company Pvt., Ltd ., (hereinafter referred 
to as the company ) Aled its written statement at 
Ex , 1 / E 
3 . According to the company , 
( i) As the cause of Shri V . Venugopalan has not 

been espoused by any workmen er ployed in 
the company no industrial dispute exists . As 
Section 2A of thc I. D . Act, 1947 is ultra vires 
the Constitution of India , reference is bad and 

not maintainable in law . 
( ii ) As Shri Venugopalan resigned from the 

service of the company, it was justificd in 
accepting his resignation . It was justified in 
not permitting Shri Venugopalan to withdraw 
his resignation after it was accepted . In these 
circumstances , it was justified in treating Shri 
Venugopalan as having resigned from the 
service of the company , and he is not entitled 

to any relief. 
4 . The affected workman Sliri Venugopalan had 
taken time for filing writton statement. He was given 
time for Aling written statement till 15th June, 1972 . 
In the meanwhile both the parties entered into settle 
ment. Settlement dated 13th June, 1972 signed by the 
parties and witnesses, Ex . 2 / EW was sent to this Tri 
bunal by post alongwith a letter , requesting the 
Tribunal to make award in terms of settlement. 

5 . On 29th June, 1972 Shri Venugopal was cxanined 
before me at Panaji. In his evidence he admits the 
settlement and to have rcccived Rs . 490 . 96 in full and 
final settlement of all his claim with the company . 

6 . Shri Devasthali, Labour Adviser of the curr . pany 
also speaks about the settlement. He identifies the 
signatures on the settlement. 

7 . Having regard to the facts of this casc and the 
evidence of the affected workman , I am of the vicw 
that the settlement arrived at between the parties is 
fair , reasonable and that it is in the interest of both 
the parties , I therefore accept the same. In the end 
I pass the following order .

एक दरखास्त के साथ एक समझोता भी पेश किया गया इस 
दरखास्त को तस्दीक दोनो पक्ष के प्रतिनिधियों ने इस अदालत 
के समक्ष की समझोता वैद्य व उचित मालुम होता है और दरखास्त 
पेश कर निवेदन किया गया है कि अब इस मामले में नो डिस्पूट 
अवार्ड पास कर दीया जावें । 

मिमल आज भारत सरकार की अधिसूचना के साथ प्राप्त 
हुई अत : 28 - 4 - 72 को फर्द अहकाम के मुताबिक इस मामले 
में ममझौता के आधार पर प्रवाई पाम किया जाता

AWARD 
By Order No. 1 / 1912 / 54 / 71 -LRIL dated 17th June , 
1971, the Government of India , in the Ministry of 
Labour , Employment & Rehabilitation (Department of 
Labour and Employment ) , referred the following 
industrial dispute existing between the employers in 
relation i to the management of Damra Colliery of 
Messrs Katras Jherriah Coal Company Limited and 
their workmen , to this Tribunal, for adjudication , 
namely : 
" Whether the refusal of work to Shri Rambodh 

Ahir , Onsetter , from the 20th May , 1970 to 
the 29th June 1970 by the management of 
Damra Colliery of Messrs Katras Jherriah 
Coal Company Limited , Post Omce Kalipahari, 
District Burdwan is justified ? If not, to what 

relief is the workmun entitled ? " 
2 . In this reference the management is present 
through Mr. D . Narsingh , Advocate . The union in 
spite of notice did not even file the written statement 
but the management did file their written statement 
as well as their documents . Notices were duly served 
on the union that espoused the cause of the workman 
in the dispute , To - day is Axed for positive hearing 
of the dispute but nobody appears on behalf of the 
workmen . 

3 . Accordingly the tribunal considers that there is 
no dispute now and renders this award as no - dispute 
award . 
Dated , 13th July , 1972 . 

( Sd .) S . N . BAGCHI, 

Presiding Officer ,

AWARD 
The Government of India , Ministry of Labour, Em 
ployment and Rehabilitation ( Department of Labour 
and Employment) by its Order No. 7 / 12 / 70 - LRII, 
dated 10th December, 1970 , referred the following dis 
pute under Section 10 ( 1 ) ( d ) of the Industrial Disputes 
Act , 1947 (hereinafter referred to as the gaid Act ) to 
adjudication by this Tribunal namely , 
" Whether the action . of the management of singa 

reni Collicries Company Limited , in refusing 
to count the previous piece - rate services of 
Sarvashri 1. Dasari Ellalah , 2 . Musala Mallaiah , 
3 . Sunkari Posham . 4 . Mudari Rajam , 5 . 
Buddsrao Chandraiah , 6 . Tombarl Lachumaiah 
7. Patha Odeloo , 8 , Palle Ruyamaloo and 9 . 
Borlakunata Durgaiah , Bank Mazdoors and 
Survashri 1. Durgam Lingaiah , 2 . Jotti Nar 
saiah and 3 . Kha Jamiyya , Bank Muçcadams 
of Morgans Pit for the purpose of granting 
service increments and for refixing wages at 


mmendations of Wage Board , is justifled ? If 

not , to what relief are the workman cntitled ? " 
This reference was taken on Ale as Industrial Dispute 
No . 11 of 1971 and notices were issued to the parties . 
For the purpose of convenience the Workmen of Sin 
gareni Collieries Company Limited , Bellampalli Divi 
sion are referred to us the Petitioners and the Singa 
reni Collieries Company Limited , Bellampalli Division 
is referred to as the respondent in the course of this 
award . 


Workers Union , Bellampalli Division filed a claims 
statement on behalf of the workmen contending that 
the claimants Sarvashri Dasari Ellaiah , 2 . Measala

4 . The matter was adjourned from time to tiine at 
the request of the parties and finally on 2nd May 1972 
a representation was made by the Vice President of 
the Union that talks of settlement were going on and 
so further time was granted to see whether the parties 
would settle the matter . On 28th June 1972 a joint 
memo of compromise was Aled stating that an award 
may be passed in terms of compromise and the same 
was recorded . The claimants contention is that they 
should be given three service increments with effect 
from 15th August 1967 taking into consideration their 
piece rate service. Whereas the Management had 
given one service increment. The contention of the 
respondent is that the piece rated service cannot be 
counted for the purpose of service increments now , 
It is secn from the memo of compromise that it was 
agreed between the parties that the respondent should 
give one extra increment in their category so far as 
eight gang mazdoors are concerned and so far as three 
gang muccadams are concerned the parties had agreed 
that since their service of three years was already 
reckoned and since they were given one increment 
correctly there was no other relief that could be 
granted to them . I am satisfied that the compromise 
entered into between the parties is fair , considering 
the nature of the dispute . 

5 . In the result an award is passed in terms of the 
compromise and a copy of the memo of compromise 
shall be attached to the Award .

the seal of this Tribunal, this the 1st day of July , 1972 .

8 . The disputed steel rope was found in the posses 
stop of witness No. 7 . According to the management this 
steel rope was stolen from the ropcway haulalo room . 
It inay be presumed that a man who is in possession of

less he can account for his possession . The theft is 
alleged to have been cominitted on 14th February , 1971 
and the stolen property was found in the possession of

AWARD 
The present reference arises out of Order No. L / 2013 / 
145 / 71 -LRII dated New Delhi, the 11th November . 
1971 passed by the Central Government in respect of an 
industrial dispute between the parties mentioned above . 
The subject matter of the dispute has been specified in 
the schedule to the said order and the sald schedule 
runs as follows: 
" Whether the employers in relation to West Bokh 

aro Colllery of Messrs West Bokharo Limited , 
Post Ofce Ghatoland , District Hazaribagh , 
were justified in dismissing Shri A . K . Sarkar , 
Head Welder from services with effect from the 
1st April 1971 ? It not, to what relief the 
workmen is entitled ? " 


9 . The explanation of witness No. 7 is that the steel 
rope in question was sold to him by the concerned work 
man af about 10 A . M . on 14th February , 1971, which was 
a Sunday . Witness No. 7 told witnesses Nos. 1 and 2 
that he had paid Rs. 40 / - to the concerned workman 
as the price of tlie steel rope . Witness No. 6 ( Six ) 
also says that at the preliminary enquiry held 
on 16th February , 1971 witness No. 7 told 
to Mr. Banerjee who held the preliminary en 
quiry that the concerned workman had given him the 
rope on payment of Rs. 40 . But in his examination in 
Chief before thc Enquiry Oficer he said that the con 
( crncil workman asked Rs. 8 ( for the rope , that he paid 
Rs. 40 and promised to pay the balance of Rs. 40 on 
recclving receipt for the same. He is not consistent 
about the price . Morcover it is dificult to believe that 
he paid Rs. 40 to the concerned workman without 
taking any receipt. 


10 . At first he says that he could not give the con 
cerned workman the price as Pantaki Babu was not at 
the site . Then he says, " At about 10 A . M . I gave Rs. 40 
as price of the rope at that time and rest of Rs. 40 I 
promised to give him when he will give me the receipt 
for the same" . IIc does not say from where he got the 
money to pay the price . 


11. He has further said in his examination in chief 
that the concerned workman came to him at the site on 
15th February , 1971 and asked for the balance of Rs. 40 
when he told him that the loss of the stcel ropc had 
lieen detcrted by the company . But this fact he did 
not disclose to witness No. 1 . Sri S . J . Banerjee when 
the latter held a preliminary enquiry on 16th February , 
1971. 


2 . The concerned workmen has been dismissed from 
service on a charge of theft. According to the manage 
ment, steel wire rope of about 49 ft . was found mis 
sing in the morning of 15th February , 1971 from the 
ropeway haulage room and later it was found being used 
in the concerete mixure machine of M / s . Solis Cons 
truction Company , and on enquiry from one Jawahir 
Thakur, Machine Driver it was learnt that the stoel ropo 
was purchased from Shri A . K . Sarkar (workman con 
cerned ) on 14th February , 1971. 

3 . A chargesheet dated 18th February , 1971 was issul 
ed to the concerned workman charging him with steal 
ing company s material and selling the same to Shri 
Jawahir Thakur . The concerned workman submitled 
his explanation dated 26th February , 1971 denying the 
charge . 

4 . A departmental enquiry was held in the presence 
of the concerned workmen who was given the opportu 
nity of cross -examining the witnesses produced by the 
management and who , in fact , cross - examined many of 
them . 

5. The Enquiry Officer found the concerned workman 
guilty of the charge levelled against him and submitted 
his report accordingly . The concerned workman was 
dismissed from service with effect from 1st April, 1071 . 

6 . The validity of the dismissal has been challenged 
by thr union on various grounds, one of them being 
that the charge rould not be established against th ? 
worloman concerned . 

7 . First of all I should like to consider whether the 
evidence before the domestic enquiry justifleg the find 


12. The above analysis of his evidence as to the pay 
ment of price makes it quite clear that he did not pay 
anything as the price of the steel rope . 

13 . In the heginning he has stated that when the con 
cerned workman gave him the steel rope on 14th 
February . 1971 he told the latter not to give him stolen 
material. It seems he recelved the steel wire, knowing 
it to be stolen . 


14 . Witness No . 6 has told something very significant. 
I am quoting the relevant portion : " On 12th February , 
1971 I was in II shift . . . . . . . . At about 7 P . M . I went to 
Gurubux Singh Tea Shop to take tea , while I was 
Telurning from the tea shop , the concerete Mixture 
Driver (Witness No. 7 ) asked me whether I will give 
him the rope which is lying in the haulage room . I 
told that it is not possible " . So witness No . 7 had an 
nye on the steel rope in question even before the inci 
dant. Witness No . 5 also says that the witness No . 7 
told something about Dichoo Orang . 

15 . It is clear that the conduct of witness No. 7 is not 
aloye suspicinn . It is highly probable in the facts and

that the union had not filed any written statement. 
To this Mr, Multherjes submitted that the union liad , 
therefore , admitted thie management s case that no 
dispilte was raised either by the workman or work 
man or by the union representing the workmun re 
garding the subject matter of the dispute before the 
managenient at any time, and that the union ap 
proached the Concillation offiier without first raising 
the dispute before the management which did not 
Convert the alleged disputę into an industrial dispute 
111 view of the decision in the case of Sindhu Resettle 
ment Corporation Ltd ., and Industrial Tribunal, 
Gujarat & Ors , reported in 1968 I LLJ p . 834 and 
Fedders Lloyd Corporation Private Ltd . and Lt. 
Goycrnor, Delhi and Ors ., reported in F . L . R . 1970 ( 20 ) 
p 343 , former by the Supreme Court of India and the 
latter by the Delhi High Court. The learned advocate , 
Mr. Bhattacherjee , for the Union submitted that the 
workman was present at the Tribunal room and that 
he would counter the statement of the management by 
inaking a statement before the Tribunal. To this 
Mr, Mukherjee submitted that Rule: 10B of the Inulusu 
rial Disputes ( Central) Rules, 1957 would not permit 
the workmani to nike out any case for the first time 
On Witness box Countering the nangement s Case on 
the point, Rule 10B ( 1 ) reads as follows: 
" Where tie Cr ntral Government refers any indus 

trral dispute for adjudication to a Labour 
Court , Tribunal or National Tribunal, within 
two weeks of the date of receipt of the oriler 
of reference , the party representing workmen 
( or in the case of individual workman , the 
workman himself ) and the employer involved 
in the dispute shall file with the Labour Court, 
Tribunal ur National Tribunal, as the case inay 
be , a stiltenient of the demands relating only 
to the issues as are included in the order of 
reference and shall also forward & copy of 
such statement to cach one of the oppusite 

parties involved in the said dispute ." 
Referring to this rule Mr, Mukherjee submitted that 
the union representing the workman was bound to 
file under the rules . a statement of case as the manage 
mont did . After the management had filed its siute 
ment of case which was not traversed by any writleri 
statement filed by the union , the management filed un 
additional written statement which also was 1100 
countered by the union by filing a rejoinder. Mr. 
Mukherjee further subinitted that the tribunal itsell 
on 15th June 1972 allowed the union su0 7010 to 
file its statement of case within 25th June. 1972 but 
the union did 10t take any step for filing the state . 
ment although on 15th June, 1972 in presence of the 
learned Advocate representing the union , the addi 
tional written statement of the nianagement was ac 
cepted wherein it was specifically stated that hefore 
raising the dispute with the Conciliation officer neither 
the workman or the workmen nor the union represen 
ting the workman approached the management with 
the dispute relating to the subject inutter under rete 
rence wherefor the dispute could not be considered in 
law as an industrial dispute . It is clear from sub 
rule ( 1 ) of rule 10B of the Industrial Dispuics 
( Central) Rules , 1957 that SO) soon as the union 20t 
the order of reference in this case ( independent ut 
the notice calling upon it to Alle its statement of de 
mand relating to the dispute under reference ) , the 
union was required to file its written statement. It 
did not take any step on 15th Junc, 1972 , 25th June, 
1972 and 29th June, 1972 to filc any statement of case 
with the clear knowledge of the contention raiscd by 
the management in its additional written statement. 
The rule, being obligatory , upon the union to file its 
statement of demand with an advance copy duly 
scrved on the managernent, the union cannot on the 
date of hearing , without disclosing its case produce 
the workran , and through him , make out a case for 
the first time without any previOLIS llotice of such case 
to the management. Both the management and the 
union 21 n required under Sub - rule ( 1 ) of Rule 101 


to file their statement of demand relating to the issue 
under reference before the tribunal within two weeks 
from the date of receipt of the order of reference for 
warding a copy of such statement to cach one of the 
opposite Parties involved in the dispute i.c . the 
management on the union and the union on the 
managcncnt. Suh - rule ( 2 ) of the Rule 10B further 
says that after the statement by the management and 
the union are filed with the Tribunal, cither of the 
parties may filc rejoinder to counter the statement of 
either of the parties as the case may be and that ilso 
williin two weeks from the date of the filing of the 
statement before the tribunal by the parties concerned . 
These rules are mandatory and are part of the law as 
has been laid down by their Lordships of tlle Suprema 
Court in the case of Delhi Cloth & General Mills Ltd ., 
Ys their workmen , reported in 1972 I LLJ p .99 . The 
rulcs must , therefore, bo obeyed and violation of the 
rule would be illegal. Therefore , the union had no 
right at this stage to produce the workmun , and 
througlı him to make out a case to counter the case of 
the management Orally on the witness box for the 
11: 31 time without any previous notice as to what the 
Lise of the uniun would be in opposition to the case 

i stated in the additional writteni statement filed by 
tile management in presence of the lawyer icpresent 
ing the union without any objection , and accepted by 
the tribunal, Therefore , as contended by Mr. Mukher 
jce for the management, the management s case in 
the additional written statement must be accepted as 
has not been denied by the union representing the 
workman inspite of tribunal s direction given to the 
union on 15th Junc, 1972 to Ale its statement of case 
wilen the additional writtcii statement of the mariage 
ment was accepted in preserice of the learned Adlvo 
cate representing the union , Arcordingly , this tribu 
not holds tliat neither the workman nor the union 
representing the workman raised any dispute relatiog 
to the subject inalter under reference with tlic 
management before approaching the Conciliation 
Officer i. e . the Government with the dispute which on 
failure of conciliation has been referred to this tribli 
nal by the Government as an industrial dispule for 
adjudication . In view of the law which is now settled 
by the decisions of the Supreme Court to which Mr. 
Mukherjee made a reference , the dispute in questioni 
referred to for adjudication is not an indusírial dis 
pute under Section 2 ( lc ) of the Industrial Disputos 
Act . Accordingly , this tribunal cannot entertain the 
dispute under reference as an industrial dispute and 
cannot adjudicate upon it. The reference is therefore , 
rejected . 
This is my award .

AWARD 


This is a petition Aled under Section 33A of the 
Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 (hercinafter referred to 
as the said Act ) for directing the respondent to rein 
state the petitioner with back wages from 23rd 
December , 1971 , 


This is a pctition filed under Section 33A of the 
Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 (hereinafter referred to 
as the said Act ) for directing the respondent to lein 
state the petitioner with back wages from 23rd Decem 
ber , 1971. 

2 . The petitioner in his petition contended as 
follows; The Management dismissed the petitioner 
without conducting proper enquiry . Fair opportunity 
was not given to him to produce the witnesses to 
defend his case and the Management has dismissed the 
petitioner with a grudge that he is an active worker 
of Sigareni Collicries Workers Union . There is no 
proof that the petitioner deliberately obstructed the 
fillers of machinc No . 2 from going to 43 dip , which 
is the main charge against the petitioner , The 
management issued charge sheet to six fillers includ 
ing the petitioner but it had taken action only against 
two fillers including the petitioner , which is nothing 
but discrimination . The enquiry is not fair and the 
enquiry officer put leading questions and cross 
examined the petitioner and the enquiry officer wrote 
wrong things which the petitioncr never stated . The 
action of the Management in dismissing the petitioner 
is wrongful, illegal and against the principles of 
natural justice and so the petitioner should bc rcin 
stated with back wages , 

3 . The respondent in its counter contended that the 
petitioner fully participated in the enguiry , that full 
opportunity was given to him , that the enquiry officer 
after carefully going through the matter subinitted 
his report , that there has been no breuch of principles 
of natural justice and victimisation and that the peti 
tion filed by the respondent under Section 33 ( 2 ) (b ) 
of the said Act may be hcard in the first instance . 

4 . After the Aling of the counter the petition was 
posted for enquiry to 5th July , 1972 . On that dato il 
petition was Aled by the petitioner through the Vice 
President of Singareni Collieries Workers Union , who 
had attested the petition , requesting for permission to 
withdraw the petition flled by the petitioner . In the 
petition it is stated that the Menagement considered 
his case sympathetically and that the Management 
had agreed to take him on his previous job as filler 
and that there is also no claim for arrears of wages 
and so he may be permitted to withdraw the petition , 
In view of the reasons urged in the present petition 
fled for permission to withdraw the petition , the 
petitioner is permitted to withdraw the main petiticn . 

Award is passed accordingly . 

Dictated to the Stenographer , transcribed by him 
and corrected by me and given under my hand and 
the seal of this Tribunal, this the 6th day of July , 
1972 . 

( Sd .) P . S . ANANTHI , 

Industrial Tribunal.

का०मा० 2095, - - यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि 
इससे उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में मैसर्स 
खांडेलवाल फैरो एलाएज लिमिटेड की जूनेवानी मेंगनीज माइन , 
डाकघर , जूनेबानी , जिला नागपुर के प्रबन्ध से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों 
और उनके कर्मकारों के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है ; 

और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के 
लिए निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है ; 

अतः, अब , प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1957 का 
14 ) की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( घ ) द्वारा प्रदत्त 
शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्द्वारा उक्त 
विवाद को उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 7-क के अधीन गठित केन्द्रीय 
सरकार औद्योगिक प्रधिकरण ( संख्या 2 ) मुम्बई को न्यायनिर्णयन 
के लिए निर्देशित करती है ।

का० प्रा० 2096.--- यतः केन्द्रीय मरकार की राय है कि 
इससे उपाबद्ध , अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में मैसर्स कोल 
प्रॉडक्ट्स प्राईवेट लिमिटेड को विक्टरी कोलियरी ( जी० एल० 
ग्रुप ) , डाकघर गोगला, जिला बर्दवान के प्रबन्ध से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों 
और उनके कर्मकारों के बीच एक औद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है ; 

और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन 
के लिए निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है ; 

अतः , अब प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम, 1947 ( 1947 का 
14 ) की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( घ ) द्वारा प्रदत्त 
शक्तियों का पयोग करते हुए, केन्द्रीय सरकार पतवाग उक्त 
विवाद को उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 7- क के अधीन गठित केन्द्रीय 
सरकार प्रौद्यपि अधिकरण , कलकत्ता को न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए 
निर्देशित करती है । 

अनुसूची 
" क्या मैमर्स कोल प्रॉडक्ट्म प्राईवेट लिमिटेड की विक्री 

कोलियरी ( जी० एल० ग्रुप ) डाकघर गोगला , 
जिला बर्दवान के प्रबन्धमण्डल का , श्री मारमन

18 . There can be no doubt from the above mentioned 
seniority list of the Drivers that at the material time 
Shri Anjilla was the junior inost driver . 


19 . As the company sold one of its car s driven by 
Shri Anjilla it retrenched the junior most driver 
namely Shri Anjilla with effect from 31st January , 
1970 , tendering him retrenchment compensation and 
notice pay , which he refused to accept. 

20 . It is clear from the record that Shri Anjilla was 
retrenched from service because he was the junior 
most driver . In retrenching him , the company has 
followed the principle of last come Arst po . It has 
also complied with the provisions of Section 25F of the 
I. D . Act , 1947 . It cannot be therefore said that pro 
visions of Sectioni 25F and Section 25G of the I . D . Act 
were not complied with in this case and that Shri 
Anjilla s retrenchment is invalid . 


25 . Shri Gadkari for the Union subnuts before me 
that the intention of the company in retrenching Shri 
Anjilla Driver was to reduce the expenditure and that 
on account of this the company was found to give 
notice of change as required under Section 9A of the 
I . D . Act , 1947. As such notice was not given in this 
case , the action of the company in rationalising the 
working force of drivers amounts to illegal change 
And the retrenchment arising on account of illegal 
change is itself illegal. 

26 . In support of the contention raised by Shri 
Gadkari in his arguments as well as in the written 
statement he relies on Section 9A of the I . D . Act , 1947 
rend with Item 10 and 11 in Fourth Schedule to the 
Act. 
27 . Section 9A of the I. D . Act, 1947 is as follows : 
" 9 - A . Notice of change - No employer who pro 

poses to effect any change in the conditions 
of service applicable to any workmen in 
respect of any matter specified in the Fourth 
Schedule shall effect such change- - 
( a ) without giving to the workmen likely to be 

affected by such change a notice in the pres 
cribed manner of the nature of the change 

proposed to be effected , or 
(by within twenty - one days of giving such 

notice provided that no notice shall be re 

quired for effecting any such change . 
( a ) where the change is effected in pursuance 

of any settlement, award or decision of the 
Appellate Tribunal constituted under th 
Industrial Disputes ( Appellate Tribunal 

Act , 48 of 1950 ; or 
( b ) where the workmen likely to be affecte 

by the change are person to whom th 
Funlamental and Supplementary Rule 
Civil Services ( Classification , control an 
Appeal) Rules . Civil Service Regulation 
Civilians in Defonce Services , (Classifict 
tion , Control Appeal) Rules or the India 

Railway Establishment Code or any othe 
rules or regulations that may be notified i 

this hehalt by the appropriate Governmer

pany has not employed any new permanent driver 
after May , 1971 ,

ditions of service. It is not necessary to dis

29 . The definition of rationalisation given in Oxford 
Illustrated Dictionery is as follows:-- - 


" Rationalize — Rcorganize ( industry etc ) on scienti 

fic lines , with elimination ot waste of labour , 
time, and materials and reduction oľ other 
costs." 


30 . On reading Section 9A of thc I. D . Act, referred 
to above it is clcar that notice of change would be e 
quired in this case if the retrenchment of Shri Anjilla 
on the ground that he became surplus as one of the 
cars which he was driving was disposed of, amounts 
to change in the conditions of his service , 


Dealing with the scope of S .31 ( 1 ) of, the Act liela 

that an enquiry into the BONA FIDES of re 
trenchment in such cases will be beyond the 
jurisdiction of the criminal court as it has 110 
power under the Industrial Disputes Act to 
look into the validity or otherwise of the 
retrenchment by the management. 

Specific 
power for the purpose has been given to the 
Industrial Tribunal in a reference made under 
S . 10 of the Act . If the termination of service 
was a colourable exercise of the power or was 
a result of victimization or unfair labour prac 
tice it was for the Industrial Tribunal which 
would have the jurisdiction to intcrvene anc 
set aside such termination ." 


31. Shri Mokoshi for the company contends that 
fourth schedule has to be read alongwith Section 9A 
and not independently . He further submits that as 
the car driven by Shri Anjilla was sold on 30th 
January . 1970 and as Shri Anjilla became surplu : 
and as he was retrenched on this account on 319t. 
January , 1970 , it cannot be said that this amounts to 
change in the conditions of service . He further sub 
mits that Section 9A does not apply to the present 
casc as there is no change in the conditions of service 
of other persons, In suport of this he relies on the 
ruling reported in 1970 , II,LLI, Page 611 betweeti 
L . M . Thapar and others and State of Bihar . 


33 . There can be no doubt from the above mention 
ed rulills that the retrenchment cannot ordinarily 
amount to an alteration in the conditions of service , 
and that lite expression conditions of service really 
implied the actual continuance of the relationship of 
the employer and employee and once the service of 2 
particular emplo " e is terminated there is no question 
of alteration in the conditions of service . 


32 . The ruling referred to above lays down as fol 
lows : - - 


34 . In the present case after the services of Shri 
Anjilla was r .freiched , the rclationship of employer 
and employee did not continue and there was no 
question of alteration in the conditions of his service. 
This ruling clear :;; supports the contention of Shri 
Mokashi. 


" In the Instant case the petitioners were prosecut 

ed under $ ,31 ( 1 ) of the Industrial Disputes 
Act, 1947 for the alleged violation of S . 33 ( 1 ) 
( a ) of the Industrial Disputes Act on the 
allegation that during the pendency of con 
ciliation proceedings before the conciliation 
officer , the pctitioner s retrenched 276 work 
men thereby altering the conditions of ser 
vicc of the workmen . 


35 . The learned Advocate Shri Gadkari contends 
that the reduction in the number of cars of the com 
pany for saving expenditure has resulted in the l e 
trenchment of Shri Anjilla and that this amounts to 
rationalisation within the meaning of item 10 in ile 
fourth Schedule , He therefore submits that such 
charge requires a notice under Section 9A to 11 
kiven . I am unable to accept this contention . If we 
read the whole expression given in item No . 10 it 
means rationalisation of plant or technine which is 
Jikely to lead to retrenchment of workmen . What I 
understand from this is that if computer is used in any 
office and on account of this the workmen are likely 
to be reduced it would amount to change in service 
cunditions of the employees . Hence notice in such 4 .35€ 
required , and not in the case which I am deciding, 
By retrenching Shri Anjilla the services conditions of 
other employces have not been affected . 


Allowing the writ petition and quashing the pro 

secution , held that in the Industrial Dispuies 
Act there is nothing that letters the right to 
retrench and it is for the management to de 
cide the strength of its labour force, for the 
number of workmen required to carry ou ! 
efficiently the work involved in the indus 
trial undertaking of any employer has to be 
detcrimined by the management in its discic 
tion and when there is an occasion when the 
number of employees has exceedcd the 
reasonable and legitimate requirements of 
the undertaking and if some workmen 
become surplus it is open to the manage 
ment to retrench them on the terms 
and conditons as provided by S . 25F , of tire 
Act, Retrenchment cannot ordinarily amount 
to an alternation in the conditions of service . 
The expression conditions of service really 
implied the actual continuance of the relation 
ship of employer and cmployees and once the 
service of a particular employee is terminated 
there is no question of alteration in the con 


36 . The learned Advocate Shri Gadkari contends 
that on account of retrenchment of Shri Anjilla , the 
number of drivers employed or to be employed in the 
Occupation of the company was reduced , and that this 
reduction was not occasioned by circumstances over 
which the cmployer has bo control. Fle therefore 
contends that the present case falls under Item 11 of 
the Fourth Schedule and that on account of this it 
amounts to change in conditions of service of other 
employecs . This item number 11 has to be read with 
Section 9A . It cannot be read independently of 
Section 9A of the I. D . Act , 1947 ,

37, Requirement of notice to workmen would ise

13 . It is contended ili the rojoilider Ex, 3 , E , para . 4

37 . A . It we read Section 9A of the I .D . Act, 1947 
and item il in fourth schedule in the light of the 
judgement relied upon by Shri Mokashi referred to 
above, it will be clear that it cannot be said in this 
casc that on account of retrenchment of Shri Anjilli 
other workmen were likely to be affected prejudi 
cially . Hence in the present case it cannot be suid 
that there was any change in the scrvice Cor:ditions 
of the employees , on account of reduction in the 
number of the employees employed or to be einploy 
ed by the company and that notice under Section 9A 
read with item 11 of the fourth schedule was meccs 
Sary . 


44 . In short, it will be clear from the above dis 
cussions that Shri Anjilla was retrenched by the 
company because he was found surplus after the sale 
of the car No. MRC - 5707 and that his retrenchment 
was proper and legal.

Unio1 contends that after the retrechment of Shri

and that on Account of this the service conditions of 
other Drivers were changed . This contention can 
not be also accepted . 


45 . As the action of the company in retrenching 
Shri Anjillal was justified , Shri Anjilla is not entitled 
to any relict . Ilence my findings on point numbers

ORDER 


39. Shri Andrew P . Serrão . Ex 4 W is the Head 
Clerk in Eagle Star Insurance Co . Ltd ., Bombay . He 
is an office bearer of tlie General Insurance Emp 
loyees Union . His evidcicc shows that one Driver 
out of four made complaint to thc Union that the 
workload had incrcased what he wants to say is 
that on account of retrenchment of Shri Anjilla thie 
remaining four drivers in the company had to do 
more work and on account of this there was change 
in the service conditions of other employees . 


( i) It is hereby declared that Eagle Star Insu 

rance Company Limited was justified in re 
trenching Shri A . A , Anjilla , Driver with 
effect fiom 31st January , 1970 and that Shri 

Anjilla is not entitled to any relief. 
( ii ) Award is made accordingly . 
( iii ) No order as costs .

S . O . 2102 . - Whereas the Central Goverment is of 
opinion that an industrial dispute exists between the 
enployers in relation to the management of Messrs 
Dehri Rohtas Light Railway Company Limited , Dalmia 
nagar And their workmen in respect of tie matters 
specified in the Schedule hrreto annexeri;

41. In the first place the Union has not produced 
the written complaint made to it in 1970 on record . 
In the absence of this written complaint alleged to 
have bech received by the Union , it is difficult to 
attach ariy weight to tlie testimony of this witness 
when he says that the complaint regarding workloal ! 
was made in writing to the Union some time in 1970 , 
If the Union would have received written complaint 
from the Drivers regarding increase in the workloa ! 
on account of retrenchment of Shri Anjilla , the Union 
would not have failed to make written representation 
to the company in this respect. In the absence of 
written grievance to the company , no weight ( all be 
attached to the testimony of the witness that he 
made oral representation to the company regarding 
increase in the workload for other drivers , 


Now therefore, in exercise of the power s conferred 
by clause ( d ) of sub - section ( 1 ) of section 10 of the 
Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 ( 14 of 1947 ) , the Central 
Governnient herely reters the said dispute for adjudi 
cation to the Industrial Tribunal ( No . 1 ) , Dhanbari 
constituted under section A of the said Act .

का० प्रा० 2104.--- यतः इससे उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट प्रौद्योगिक विवाद श्री गोपाल नारायण शर्मा, पीठासीन अधिकारी, औद्यो 
गिक अधिकरण, जयपुर , के समक्ष लमित है ; 


और यत : श्री गोपाल नागयण शर्मा की मेवायें अप उपलब्ध नहीं रहीं हैं । 


अतः, अप औद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम 1947 ( 1947 का 14 ) की धारा 7 क और 33 ख की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों 
का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्वारा एक प्रौद्योगिक अधिकरण गठित करती है जिसके पीठासीन अधिकारी श्री मोहम्मद याकूब खां 
होंगे , जिनका मुख्यालय जयपुर ; होगा , श्री गोपाल नागयण शर्मा के समक्ष लम्ति उक्त विवाद से सम्बद्ध कार्यवाहियों को वापस लेती 
है और उसे उक्त प्रौद्योगिक प्रधिकरण जयपुर को , जो श्री मोहम्मद याकूब खां को उसका पीठासीन अधिकारी बनाते हुए गठित किया गया 
है , अन्तरित करती है और निदेश देती है कि उक्त प्रधिकरण उक्त कार्यवाहियों पर उमी प्रक्रम से कार्यवाही करेगा जिस पर वह उसे अन्तरित 
की गई है और विधि के अनुसार उसका निपटान करेगा ।

New Delhi, the 25th July 1972 
S . O . 2107 . - --In exercise of the powers conferred 
by sub - section ( 1 ) of section ID of the Waki Act , 1954 
( 29 of 1954 ) , the Central Government hereby makes 
the following rules to amend the Central Wakf Council 
Rules, 1963 , namely :- - 
___ 1 . ( 1 ) These rules may be called the Central wakl 
Council ( Amendment ) Rules , 1972 . 

(2 ) They shall come into force on the date of 
their publication in the Official Gazette . 

2 . In the Central Wakf Council Rules , 1965 ( herein 
after referred to as the said rules ), in rule 8 , after sub 
rule ( 2 ) , the following sub - rule shall be inserted . name 
ly : - - 
" ( 3 ) The Chairman may , at hls discretion , grant 

travelling allowance and daily allowance to 
any special invitee to any meeting of the 
Council or its Committee or Sub - Committee 

at the rate admissible to a member ." . 
3 . In rule 9 of the said rules, after sub -rule ( ?? ) , lhc 
following sub -rule shall be inserted , namely : 
" (3 ) ( a ) A member, resident at a place here the 

meeting of the Council is held , will not be 
entitled to travelling allowance and dally 
allowance for attending the meeting of the 
Council, but only to actual cost of conveyance 
hired subject to a maximum of ten rupecs per 

day . 
( b ) The controlling officer shall verify the clajm 

and satisfy himsell that the actual expenditure 
was not less than the amount claimed . Where 
the controlling officer is not satisfied with the 
details , he may . at his discretion, limit the 

cost of conveyance hired 10 road milage , 
( c ) If the member uses his own car , he will be 

granted milage allowance at the rates admis 
sible to an official of the first grade subject to 

a maximum of ten rupces per day.” 
4 . In rule 11 of the said rules , after sub - rule ( 2 ), the 
following sub -rules shall be inserted , namely : 
“ ( 2A ) The appointing authority of the employees 

of the Council shall be the discipuinary autho 
rity and shall be competent to impose all kinds 

of punishments including dismissal. 
(2B ) In cases of disciplinary proceedings against 

the employees of the Council , where the dis 
ciplinary authority is the Chairman , the Coun 
oil shall be the appellate authority and where 
the disciplinary authority is the Secretary, the 

Chairman shall be the appellate authority . 
( 2C ) The Secretary shall be competent to make 

appointment in regard to the post of Lower 
Division Clerk and he shall also be the autho 
rity competent to order removal of, or impose 

punishment on a person holding such post." 
5 . After rule 12 of the said rules, the following rule 
shall be inserted , namely : - - 
" 12A . Sanction of expenditure by Chairman and 

Secretary . — ( 1) Subject to the provisions of 

the budget, - 
( a ) the Chairman shall sanction a recurring 

expenditure upto one thousand rupees , and 
a non - rccurring expenditure of two thousand 
rupecs , on each item per annum , 
the Secretary shall sanction a reurring ex 
penditure upto hundred and fifty rupees , 
and a non -recrurring expenditure ip to 
five hundred rupees , on each item per annum .

आयकर 
नई दिल्ली , 11 जुलाई 1972 
एस०प्रो 2108. - -- प्रायकर अधिनियम 1961 ( 1961 का 43 ) 
की धारा 2 के खण्ड ( 4 3ख ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते 
हए और इस विषय से संबंधित सभी पूर्व प्रादेशों को अतिष्ठित करते 
हुए, केन्द्रीय सरकार, इस अधिसूचना म संलग्न मारणी में विनिर्दिष्ट 
आयकर आयुक्तों और अपर ग्रायकर आयुक्तां को , कर वसुली 
पायुक्तों की शक्तियों का प्रयोग करने के लिए एतद्द्वारा प्राधिकृत 
करती है । 


CENTRAL BOARD OF DIRECT TAXES 

CORRIGENDUM 

New Delhi, the 18th March , 1972 
S . O . 2109 . - --In exercise of the powers conferred by 
sub - section ( 1 ) of section 122 of the Income- tax Act, 
1961 ( 43 of 1961) and of all other powers enabling it 
in that behalf, then Central Board of Direct Taxes , 
hereby directs that the following correction shall be 
made in its Notification No. 54 ( F .No. 261 / 1 / 72 - ITJ) , 
dated 6th March , 1972 . 

In the last line of the Notification , for 6th Marcii , 
1972 read 9th March, 1972 . 

[ No. 61 ( F . No. 261 / 1 / 72 -ITI. ] 

P . K . SHARAN , Under Secy .

का 43 ) की धारा 1 2 2 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का

उसकी अधिसूचना मं० 54 ( फा० सं० 261/ 1/ 72- आई०टी० 
जे० ) तारीख 6 मार्च , 1972 में निम्नलिखित शुद्धि की जाएगी :

9- 3- 1972 पढ़ा जाए ।
